HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0ebd5830b42552f5fd6f","text":"2. **`الدين` as the horizon of enacted obedience.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `د ي ن` B001 includes obedience, submission, worship, and the way one follows; 1:5 `ع ب د` B003 is worship as submissive obedience. **Structural cue:** the sovereign title is followed immediately by `إِيَّاكَ نَعْبُدُ`, with the object fronted for exclusivity. **Role and abductive move:** the fixed branch names the relation and the next ayah enacts it; I infer that the coming “day” is already allowed to govern present allegiance. **Reading change:** `يوم الدين` can coexist with reckoning as the horizon on which submission is disclosed, while 1:5 is its present rehearsal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_18866b4688bfcf9c0ff8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:4:3\",\"root_000504\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Alınan merhamet, yardım ve iyilik, günü yalnız cezanın değil, bunların doğurduğu yükümlülüğün de hesaba katıldığı bir olay olarak açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2b2244db43f5a9587914","text":"1. **The severe event-day of reckoning under dominion.** Evidence: `م ل ك B003` kingship/dominion, `ي و م B003` event-day or severe occasion, and `د ي ن B002` reckoning/recompense. Mechanism: the three roots lock into a forensic-sovereign scene: dominion specifies who controls the event, يوم gives the occasion weight, and دين supplies accounting. The packet supplies these three elements; I infer their convergence into a tribunal-like mechanism. Abductive move: when “day” is paired with recompense rather than ordinary sequence, it becomes an event of settlement. Reading change: the ayah reads as ownership of the decisive occasion, not only ownership on a calendar day.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_369254fa07c3140ed949","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:4:1\",\"root_001444\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sonraki yol isteğiyle sahiplik, yolun ana yatağını elinde tutan ve onu yan kollardan ayıran bir merkez olma boyutu kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B002","root_000532/B001","root_001273/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_39ca2c1472020154802e","text":"4. **Standing before the owner.** Evidence: `ق و م B013` resurrection and people standing before their Lord, activated by `ق و م` in “المستقيم” at 1:6; `ر ب ب B001` lordship; `د ي ن B002` reckoning. Mechanism: the later straight-standing root carries a resurrection branch backward into يوم الدين: the path of uprightness anticipates standing before the Lord. The packet supplies the standing/resurrection branch and the reckoning frame; I infer temporal reversal from future standing back to present path. Abductive move: a straight path matters because it trains or leads to the final standing. Reading change: “يوم الدين” is not only an external future event; it presses backward into present posture.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B002","root_001700/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3d0e9e9535c271ab7fe8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:4:2\",\"root_001700\",[\"B003\"]],[\"1:4:3\",\"root_000504\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Daha sonra istenen doğruluk, hesap gününü insanın sonunda sahibinin önünde nasıl duracağıyla şimdiki duruşunu birbirine bağlayan bir ufka dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_000504/B002","root_000973/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_46c8182a6852bd1bbcc1","text":"2. **Dīn as obedience now and recompense later.** Lexical evidence: د ي ن B001, obedience/submission/law; د ي ن B002, reckoning/recompense; ع ب د B003 in 1:5, worship and submissive obedience. Structural cue: “Day of dīn” is immediately followed by “You we worship.” Assigned role: dīn links the present act of submission to a later order of reckoning. Abductive move: the packet supplies the adjacency; I infer a temporal arrow from present obedience to future accounting. Reading change: the ayah reads not as an isolated eschatological line but as the horizon under which 1:5 becomes intelligible.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_576fd4ad2fab18c1e95b","text":"1:6’s path language unexpectedly activated م ل ك B006, the main course of road or valley. That made مالك less exclusively regal and more axial: the Owner of the Day also owns the centerline by which movement and judgment are held together.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_60f1b55b18d246ccca38","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:4:3\",\"root_000504\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hesap, yalnız gelecekteki karşılığı değil, şimdiki kulluğu yöneten itaat ufkunu da ayete taşır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_001273/B009","root_001444/B005","root_001444/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7498917aade1ed897944","text":"4. **Malik as mainstay and center of the path-order.** Lexical evidence: م ل ك B005, mainstay of a matter; م ل ك B006, center/main course of road or valley; ص ر ط B001 in 1:6–1:7, straight road; ق و م B009 in 1:6, support/mainstay. Structural cue: the path appears after this ayah, and “straight” is built from standing/support. Assigned role: مالك becomes the central controlling support that keeps the road-order from scattering. Abductive move: the packet supplies road/center/mainstay; I infer that judgment is the axis around which the requested path is stabilized. Reading change: the ayah reads as the structural center of the surah’s movement, not only as a title of rule.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B001","root_001700/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a1e211a9bc8d3cf390c5","text":"1:5 immediately activated fixed `د ي ن` B001 by turning obedience from a lexical possibility into performed speech. Then 1:7’s differentiated outcomes sharpened `ي و م` B003 and `د ي ن` B002 into a decisive sorting event, while its `أنعمت` made the debt branch newly visible as a separate, exploratory obligation model. The retrospective therefore preserves obedience, recompense, and debt-settlement as coexisting mechanisms rather than collapsing them.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003","root_001444/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a63dcbb4a60e7f87ec73","text":"1:6–1:7 changed this ayah most: the later صراط made `م ل ك B006` visible, so ملك was no longer only royal possession but also the center or main channel of a road. 1:7’s نعمة also made `د ي ن B003` debt/credit unexpectedly relevant: the day of דין can account for received favor, not only punish wrongdoing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b3d3d02e9d9e59a03b1b","text":"3. **Debt, favor, and repayment.** Evidence: `د ي ن B003` debt/credit, `ن ع م B001` bestowed favor in 1:7, `ح م د B001` thanksgiving in 1:2, and `ع و ن B001` help in 1:5. Mechanism: favor and help create received benefit; دين as debt/recompense turns the day into a reckoning of dependence. The packet supplies favor, help, thanksgiving, and debt; I infer an economy of received bounty and answerability. Abductive move: one who receives نعمة and عون stands in a relation that must be acknowledged rather than possessed as private merit. Reading change: the ayah can read as the day when benefaction, gratitude, obligation, and recompense are brought into one account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B002","root_001444/B002","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b4dbd155b3171073cf89","text":"1. **Ownership of reckoning as custody of the whole account.** Lexical evidence: م ل ك B002, ownership and disposal; د ي ن B002, reckoning and recompense; ن ع م B001 in 1:7, bestowed favor; غ ض ب B001 in 1:7, anger toward retaliation. Structural cue: the ayah sits before the worship/help pledge and before the sorting of favored, angered, and astray. Assigned role: مالك holds the account in which favor and liability are settled. Abductive move: the packet supplies ownership, recompense, favor, and wrath; I infer an accounting mechanism whose final disposal belongs to the owner. Reading change: the Day of dīn reads as custodial settlement, not only a scene of sovereignty.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B008","root_001444/B006","root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_be49a24a243e68afff63","text":"2. **The path has a controlling center and main course.** Evidence: `م ل ك B006` center/main course of road or valley, activated by `ص ر ط B001` road/path in 1:6–1:7; `ق و م B008` straightness/uprightness; `ه د ي B001` showing the road. Mechanism: later ṣirāṭ turns ملك from sheer possession toward the “main course” that governs where one can travel. The packet supplies road, straightness, guidance, and the road-center branch; I infer that ملك can function as the central channel of the path. Abductive move: a path becomes safe when its governing center is known and not confused with side routes. Reading change: “مالك” also activates as the holder of the road’s central line, so judgment is connected to navigation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B002","root_001444/B003","root_001700/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ce0fff499ba20161609d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:4:1\",\"root_001444\",[\"B003\"]],[\"1:4:2\",\"root_001700\",[\"B003\"]],[\"1:4:3\",\"root_000504\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Hesap günü, takvimdeki sıradan bir gün değil, farklı yolların sonuçlarının egemen biçimde ayrıştırıldığı belirleyici olaydır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001444/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d6af0784c5bf04cfa01f","text":"1. **Sovereignty over a decisive sorting event.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `م ل ك` B003 is kingship and dominion, fixed `ي و م` B003 is a major or severe event-day, and fixed `د ي ن` B002 is reckoning, judgment, and recompense. **Structural cue:** 1:7 later differentiates favored people, people under wrath, and people astray. **Role and abductive move:** dominion supplies control, the event-day supplies decisive occasion, and reckoning supplies allocation; I infer that the three differentiated outcomes are consequences made visible by that reckoning. **Reading change:** the phrase presents not a date on a calendar but a controlled event in which divergent courses become consequentially sorted.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001700/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e9090db5cc631e619aef","text":"5. **The “day” as severe event rather than mere date.** Lexical evidence: ي و م B003, event-day or severe occasion; غ ض ب B001 in 1:7, intensified anger toward retaliation; ض ل ل B002/B003, disappearance/loss. Structural cue: the day is defined by dīn and later by contrasted destinies. Assigned role: يوم marks the crisis-time when hidden loss and provoked anger become manifest. Abductive move: the packet supplies severe occasion and later moral outcomes; I infer that the “day” is an event of exposure and sorting. Reading change: the ayah reads as an occurrence of decisive manifestation, not merely a calendrical period.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f2ba43a6e736ca3ade38","text":"3. **An exploratory debt-settlement mechanism.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `د ي ن` B003 is debt and credit, and fixed `م ل ك` B002 is ownership and authorized disposal. Earlier mercy and nurture, plus later aid and 1:7 `ن ع م` B001 favor, supply received benefit. **Structural cue:** benefaction, ownership, service, and accounting occur in a tight sequence. **Role and abductive move:** debt supplies an obligation model and ownership supplies standing to settle it; I explicitly infer the causal arrow that received favor creates a nonfinancial obligation answered by service and finally accounted for. **Reading change:** alongside judgment and obedience, `الدين` can make the day a settlement of what dependent recipients owe in response to what they were given.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000504/B003","root_001064/B001","root_001525/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_f5bdf54a77dc18609cc9","text":"3. **Debt and favor as an unsettled economy of dependence.** Lexical evidence: د ي ن B003, financial debt/credit; ن ع م B001, benefaction given to another; ع و ن B001 in 1:5, seeking help. Structural cue: the speaker asks for help and later names people upon whom favor was bestowed. Assigned role: dīn’s debt branch makes favor and help feel like obligations that cannot be self-settled. Abductive move: the packet supplies debt, help, and favor; I infer that dependence creates an account held by the مالك. Reading change: “Day of dīn” also reads as the day when received aid and favor are accounted for.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
